@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.

diff --git a/src/autofdtd/core/models.py b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d0326b7b65d38dc950f7b3fb9a628bc1fadabb51
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

+def test_shared_model_helpers_expose_tag_copy_and_json_ready() -> None:
+    tagged = TaggedStub(value=2)
+    updated = tagged.copy_update(value=5)
+
+    assert tagged.model_tag() == "TaggedStub"
+    assert updated.value == 5
+    assert tagged.value == 2
+    assert json_ready({"payload": tagged, "items": (1, 2)}) == {
+        "payload": {"type": "TaggedStub", "value": 2},
+        "items": [1, 2],
+    }

 - `src/autofdtd/core/containers.py` now holds the first frozen tagged container models for `Structure`, `Scene`, and `Simulation`; `src/autofdtd/api/simulation.py` re-exports them as the public API surface.
 - `src/autofdtd/ir/models.py` now defines the first versioned execution IR, with `ComponentIR`, `StructureIR`, `SceneIR`, `SimulationIR`, and `ExecutionPackageIR` as the transport contract.

 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.

+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")

 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.
 
 ## Task Handoffs

diff --git a/src/autofdtd/core/models.py b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d0326b7b65d38dc950f7b3fb9a628bc1fadabb51
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.
 
 ## Task Handoffs

diff --git a/src/autofdtd/core/models.py b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d0326b7b65d38dc950f7b3fb9a628bc1fadabb51
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d0326b7b65d38dc950f7b3fb9a628bc1fadabb51
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.

+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):

+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")

+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.
 
 ## Task Handoffs

diff --git a/src/autofdtd/core/models.py b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d0326b7b65d38dc950f7b3fb9a628bc1fadabb51
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

+- `src/autofdtd/core/models.py` now provides the shared frozen model contract with `model_tag()`, `copy_update()`, `json_ready()`, and stable JSON serialization helpers reused by both public containers and IR models.
 
 ## Decisions
 
@@ -31,6 +32,7 @@
 - Use a generic family-tagged component envelope in the IR so Phase 1 can package realistic simulations before every feature family has concrete typed IR classes; later tasks can specialize payloads without changing bundle shape.
 - Bundle packaging is directory-based: `manifest.json` points at a hashed `simulation.json` entrypoint, avoiding self-referential manifest hashing while staying suitable for remote upload or local caching.
 - Core validation is checklist-driven: implemented feature tags pass, deferred or rejected tags fail early with explicit errors, and known primitive geometry payloads validate only when enough fields are present so placeholder tagged dicts can survive until their concrete family tasks land.
+- Shared model behavior should live in `autofdtd.core.models`; public API and IR classes should subclass it rather than duplicating serialization or copy helpers in subsystem-local base classes.
 
 ## Task Handoffs

diff --git a/src/autofdtd/core/models.py b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d0326b7b65d38dc950f7b3fb9a628bc1fadabb51
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped

+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/models.py
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+"""Shared frozen model, tagging, and serialization helpers."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Self
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
+
+
+def json_ready(value: Any) -> Any:
+    """Normalize arbitrary model payloads into JSON-safe Python values."""
+    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return value.as_posix()
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return json_ready(value.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        return {str(key): json_ready(item) for key, item in value.items()}
+    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
+        return [json_ready(item) for item in value]
+    raise TypeError(f"unsupported JSON payload value: {type(value)!r}")
+
+
+class AutoFDTDModel(BaseModel):
+    """Frozen base model with copy/update and stable serialization helpers."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def model_tag(self) -> str:
+        """Return the explicit type tag when present, else the model class name."""
+        tagged_type = getattr(self, "type", None)
+        if isinstance(tagged_type, str) and tagged_type.strip():
+            return tagged_type.strip()
+        return self.__class__.__name__
+
+    def copy_update(self, **updates: Any) -> Self:
+        """Return a frozen copy with a shallow field update."""
+        return self.model_copy(update=updates)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready payload with stable field names."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots and debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the model payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+class TaggedModel(AutoFDTDModel):
+    """Shared base for immutable tagged public and IR-facing models."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="")
+
+    @field_validator("type")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
+        stripped = value.strip()
+        if not stripped:
+            raise ValueError("type tags must be non-empty")
+        return stripped
